# Deliver and read student work consistently

## Authorization and release

Joshua requests publication of the reviewed My Work polish plus reliable current/future assignment categories, sorting, deduplication, assignment notifications and opened-document read state for every student. Implement locally, independently inspect final changes with Claude Opus 5.5 Medium, then merge/publish and prove the live bundle and database state. Preserve charcoal/off-white and existing completion semantics. No student records/messages/history may be deleted, no historical inbox/email may be sent, and the unrelated dirty supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts must remain excluded. Any credit-spending Lovable action still requires immediate explicit approval; direct free frontend publication and authorized SQL migration do not authorize edge-agent spending.

## Verified starting evidence

- Prior visual/reader correction is locally committed as72077d75, independently approved with zero findings and254tests, not yet published. Integrate current origin/main before this feature and recheck the merged result.
- Live database has37students with268visible Classroom rows, one scheduled row, one concurrent duplicate bootcamp route, and two completed rows without a completion timestamp. Preserve history and do not invent dates.
- Native source rows exist without mirrors (including TA, guided and activities); practice-test mirrors use legacy practice_test:<pt> aliases. Mirror UUID alone is not a canonical identity.
- Current bell writing is split across browser helpers and MCP SQL; the assumed unread uniqueness index is absent live. Raw work feed lacks opened state and publication filtering; Home equates assigned with New; bell clicks mark read before successful destination access.
- Bundles are one notification per assignWorkBundle invocation. curriculumSchedule invokes separate day bundles; the separate useWeeklyPlanPublisher/TeacherWeeklyPlans flow sends one plan summary. Preserve both distinct contracts and retain explicit notify:false.
- pg_cron is available live. Saved schema/function evidence is in the shared design review folder my-work-assignment-sync-20261028.

## State and identity contract

Keep source assignments authoritative. Canonical work identity is student_id plus source_table, source_id (native assignment UUID) and issuance generation; normalize native mirror aliases including practice_test:<pt>. Standalone notes, bootcamps and links use classroom_items:<id> plus issuance. Add a server-owned generation counter on source/Classroom records and increment on genuine inactive/hidden/revoked to active issuance, not ordinary progress/metadata updates. Source and mirror resolution must agree on the current issuance. Practice-test upsert/reactivation must produce a new alert/read identity even when the UUID is reused; ordinary retries must not. Reassignment instances remain distinct.

Choose duplicate mirror presentation deterministically by published/valid state and stable created_at/id ordering; never let an older mirror overwrite a newer one. Collapse only an identical concurrent deliverable target/content, never two distinct guided instances, editions or instructions. The live duplicate Unless Drill rows have identical targeting metadata and content hashes; preserve the older record/history while presenting the current one. A hidden, draft, archived, revoked or future-locked mirror blocks that underlying source from leaking into lists/counts. Source-only assignments remain supported using their own ownership/publication/unlock rules.

Add owner-scoped read receipts, separate from progress/completion. A successful actual destination load records first/last opened and read atomically with clearing its associated assignment alerts. A navigation click, failed/denied/pending load, stale actor request or teacher preview cannot acknowledge another student's work. Preserve existing note read timestamps, TA viewed_at and guided read_card_at as defensible backfill evidence. Do not infer read from dismissed notifications or manufacture completion dates. Receipt writes must not replace whole note metadata or change completion/progress.

## Reliable assignment delivery

Use a durable assignment event/outbox identity with lifetime uniqueness per issuance independent of notification read status OR deletion. Store delivered_at permanently; deleting a bell must never redeliver it. Add persisted notification_policy (auto/managed/suppressed/legacy_client) and group ID to the five native source tables; use equivalent standalone Classroom metadata. New source/standalone inserts and genuine reactivation enqueue through a shared SQL helper, with canonical alias resolution preventing a source+mirror double event. Existing source statuses, ownership guards and RLS remain authoritative. New notification identity/policy/generation fields are immutable to student direct updates.

Eligible automatic Classroom types are note, homework, drill, activity, bootcamp_module, practice_test and link; weekly_plan_task has an explicit managed weekly-plan policy. session_recap, smartboard_recap and announcement default to suppressed for assignment delivery and retain their existing message channel, avoiding an extra New assignment alert. They still support owned document receipts and exact linked-notification clearing.

Client-managed bundles write policy/group at insertion, then finalize one group after successful writes. SQL MCP writers use the same helper within their transactions and honor p_notify. Auto writers (including existing practice-test edge paths) require no edge deployment. Suppressed work never delivers. Managed recovery requires a minimum age of ten minutes, beyond bounded client attempt timeout, and rechecks current source existence/publication/ownership/unlock before release. Source DELETE triggers cancel pending events; no polymorphic dangling identity may announce removed work. Existing rollback may delete only rows newly created in that failed attempt, never pre-existing assignments/history. Notification finalization is outside the rollback boundary: a notification failure after assignment commit must not remove assigned work. This documents existing intentional attempt rollback, not authorization for host deletion of student records.

Group cohorts with different unlock times cannot disclose locked future content. For scheduled assignments, suppress immediate inbox/email as well as bell; only the in-app event is delivered at unlock (no deferred email worker in this scope). Reading Center follows the same rule. For unscheduled work retain existing optional inbox/email channels without double-sending them. Centralize every notifyStudentOfClassroomItem caller, including AssignmentBuilder, TeacherBootcamps, StudentSchedulePanel, agentActions, teacherAssignResource and session bundle paths, not only assignWork.

The separate weekly-plan publisher writes managed/suppressed item policies and one persisted plan publication group. Retain one immediate generic plan-ready summary with no future task titles, descriptions or bodies, and no additional per-item unlock pings. The summary describes the published plan rather than claiming future locked items were opened. The curriculumSchedule day-bundle path keeps one notification per call, deferred to that day's unlock. Test a seven-day plan and seven day-bundle calls separately.

Legacy backfill parses only exact supported internal routes into canonical event links, ignoring query parameters safely; no title or substring matching. Audit counts for mapped versus unmappable legacy alerts. Unmappable generic /classroom and /my-work summaries retain click-to-read behavior and never synthesize item receipts. Mapped alerts defer clearing to successful destination acknowledgment.

A bounded named pg_cron job releases due eligible events, with transactional locking and retry-safe inserts. Use locked search_path, explicit function grants, ownership checks and an event uniqueness constraint. Reconcile the obsolete notifications_unique_unread_assignment index with DROP INDEX IF EXISTS; uniqueness must use durable event/group identity rather than shared links or unread state. Test both the live index baseline and a baseline with the old index present. Backfill only receipts and display/reconciliation links for current records; never generate historical pings. Preserve already-read notifications and existing messages. No record deletion is needed for deduplication.

### Compatibility rollout is two phases

First install schema/RPCs with delivery activation off. Defaults are legacy_client for formerly client-notified source/standalone writers; practice-test source defaults to auto because the existing edge writer never notifies. Legacy pre-activation rows remain legacy and do not acquire a second automatic event. Then publish the new frontend, which explicitly supplies auto/managed/suppressed policies on every supported writer. Verify the exact live bundle before activating delivery and the cron job. On activation, reject new legacy_client work inserts/reactivations from stale teacher clients BEFORE writing any new work, with an actionable Refresh LSAT U error; do not silently miss or double-notify them. Existing practice-test edge auto writes and updated SQL MCP functions continue normally. No default flip while stale clients can create unkeyed work. Existing source-backed mirrors inherit their native policy. Notes/recaps written through the shared helper or MCP specify their policy explicitly. Read-only student access is never blocked by the writer version gate. Test old cached writer behavior before and after activation, including scheduled and grouped work.

## Frontend behavior

One shared source/publication projection feeds My Work, Home and unread counts. Include practice tests; expose opened/read independently of assigned/in-progress/completed. Show clear New/Unread/Read text without colour-only state. Keep work type filters and sorting stable, with dates based on actual saved data. My Work/sidebar counts must use accessible visible work only.

A shared successful-load acknowledgment hook is wired to actual note/homework/TA, section directions, guided, activity, bootcamp/quick drill, Reading Center and practice-test destination readiness. Routes carry explicit canonical work identity/issuance where a shared destination is ambiguous; loading /testing alone never acknowledges all assigned PTs. A specific PT runner acknowledges its loaded owned PT assignment; two different PTs remain distinct. Identical concurrent bootcamp aliases resolve to the deterministic presented identity, never an unrelated target. Receipt/feed/notification cache keys and realtime invalidation are actor-scoped. Assignment bell navigation defers mapped alert clearing to destination acknowledgment; other notification types and unmapped legacy generic summaries retain their own behavior.

Single-item alert clears on that successful item load. A multi-item group alert clears after all currently delivered/visible group members have receipts, OR after the student successfully loads its explicit group collection from the alert and acknowledges that alert. The latter clears only the notification, never marks individual documents read. Remaining item unread labels/counts stay truthful. Test a three-item bundle and two PTs. Preserve actual Inbox message read logic/history; viewing a linked assignment does not falsely read an unopened conversation.

Grouped alert navigation renders an explicit owner-scoped mixed note/work collection through get_assignment_notification_group_items before acknowledging the alert. Generic My Work list loads do not clear it. Individual documents remain independently unread. Account access filtering retains the alert unread when the collection is only partially available. Native persisted started_at/completed_at are conservative historical opened evidence; status alone never fabricates a receipt. Active practice-test retries with omitted/null unlock preserve an existing future schedule. Client assignment attempts are bounded to two minutes, below ten-minute managed recovery.

new file mode 100644
index 00000000..ed2399de
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/components/my-work/WorkFilterSelect.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@
+import { useCallback, useId, useRef, useState } from 'react';
+import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';
+import { useNotesSelectMotion } from '@/hooks/useNotesSelectMotion';
+
+export function WorkFilterSelect({ label, allLabel, value, options, onChange }: {
+  label: string; allLabel: string; value: string;
+  options: Array<{ value: string; label: string }>;
+  onChange: (value: string, focusedWithin: boolean, modality: 'keyboard' | 'pointer') => void;
+}) {
+  const labelId = useId();
+  const valueId = useId();
+  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
+  const contentRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
+  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
+  const [modality, setModality] = useState<'keyboard' | 'pointer'>('keyboard');
+  const modalityRef = useRef(modality);
+  function recordModality(next: 'keyboard' | 'pointer') { modalityRef.current = next; setModality(next); }
+  const motionRef = useNotesSelectMotion(open, modality);
+  const setContentRef = useCallback((element: HTMLDivElement | null) => {
+    contentRef.current = element;
+    motionRef(element);
+  }, [motionRef]);
+  return <div className="my-work-filter-select">
+    <span id={labelId} className="sr-only">{label}</span>
+    <Select open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen} value={value || 'all'} onValueChange={(next) => {
+      const active = document.activeElement;
+      const focusedWithin = !!active && (triggerRef.current?.contains(active) || contentRef.current?.contains(active));
+      onChange(next === 'all' ? '' : next, Boolean(focusedWithin), modalityRef.current);
+    }}>
+      <SelectTrigger ref={triggerRef} aria-labelledby={`${labelId} ${valueId}`} className="notes-select-trigger" onPointerDown={() => recordModality('pointer')} onKeyDown={() => recordModality('keyboard')}><SelectValue id={valueId} /></SelectTrigger>
+      <SelectContent onPointerDownCapture={() => recordModality('pointer')} onKeyDownCapture={() => recordModality('keyboard')} ref={setContentRef} className="notes-select-content" data-input-modality={modality}>
+        <SelectItem className="notes-select-option" value="all">{allLabel}</SelectItem>
+        {options.map((option) => <SelectItem key={option.value} className="notes-select-option" value={option.value}>{option.label}</SelectItem>)}
+      </SelectContent>
+    </Select>
+  </div>;
+}

new file mode 100644
index 00000000..3e6afe05

+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { teachingNoteDate } from './teachingNoteDates';
+
+describe('teaching note calendar dates', () => {
+  it('groups a UTC month boundary in the same local month as its date label', () => {
+    const value = teachingNoteDate('2026-10-01T01:00:00Z', 'America/Toronto');
+    expect(value.key).toBe('2026-09');
+    expect(value.month).toMatch(/September/);
+    expect(value.label).toMatch(/Sep.*30/);
+  });
+  it('handles a local month ahead of UTC consistently', () => {
+    const value = teachingNoteDate('2026-09-30T23:00:00Z', 'Asia/Tokyo');
+    expect(value.key).toBe('2026-10');
+    expect(value.label).toMatch(/Oct.*1/);
+  });
+  it('uses a clear fallback for missing or malformed dates', () => {
+    for (const input of ['', 'not-a-date']) {
+      expect(teachingNoteDate(input)).toEqual({ key: 'undated', month: 'Date unavailable', label: 'Date unavailable' });
+    }
+  });
+  it.each(['fa-IR', 'th-TH'])('keeps labels and grouping Gregorian under the %s default calendar', (locale) => {
+    const DateTimeFormat = Intl.DateTimeFormat;
+    const spy = vi.spyOn(Intl, 'DateTimeFormat').mockImplementation((selectedLocale, options) => new DateTimeFormat(selectedLocale ?? locale, options));
+    try {
+      const date = new Date('2026-10-01T01:00:00Z');
+      const result = teachingNoteDate(date.toISOString(), 'America/Toronto');
+      expect(result.key).toBe('2026-09');
+      expect(result.month).toBe(new DateTimeFormat(locale, { calendar: 'gregory', timeZone: 'America/Toronto', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }).format(date));
+      expect(result.label).toBe(new DateTimeFormat(locale, { calendar: 'gregory', timeZone: 'America/Toronto', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }).format(date));
+      expect(spy.mock.calls.every(([, options]) => options?.calendar === 'gregory')).toBe(true);
+    } finally { spy.mockRestore(); }
+  });
+});
diff --git a/src/components/my-work/teachingNoteDates.ts b/src/components/my-work/teachingNoteDates.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..04b26727
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/components/my-work/teachingNoteDates.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,14 @@
+/** Archive headings and row dates must use the same calendar/time zone. */
+export function teachingNoteDate(value: string, timeZone?: string) {
+  const date = new Date(value);
+  if (!value || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
+    return { key: 'undated', month: 'Date unavailable', label: 'Date unavailable' };
+  }
+  const options: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions = { calendar: 'gregory', ...(timeZone ? { timeZone } : {}) };
+  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { ...options, year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit' }).formatToParts(date);
+  return {
+    key: `${parts.find((part) => part.type === 'year')?.value}-${parts.find((part) => part.type === 'month')?.value}`,
+    month: new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { ...options, month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }).format(date),
+    label: new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { ...options, month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }).format(date),
+  };
+}

+export type ClassroomListIntent = 'filter' | 'restore' | 'paginate';
+const listOwners = new WeakMap<HTMLElement, (intent: ClassroomListIntent) => void>();

+/** Resolve the first actual owner, including a reduced-motion owner, across portals via the caller's page ref. */
+export function notifyClassroomListIntent(element: HTMLElement | null, intent: ClassroomListIntent) {
+  for (let node = element; node; node = node.parentElement) {
+    const owner = listOwners.get(node);
+    if (owner) { owner(intent); return; }
+  }

+  });
+  it('keeps same-key permission-delayed rails immediate under reduced motion', async () => {

+    await Promise.resolve();
+    expect(state.timelines).toHaveLength(0); expect(view.root).not.toHaveAttribute('data-note-transition-active');
+    expect(window.scrollTo).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
+  });
+  it('does not read layout again on an unrelated same-route render', () => {
+    const view = fixture();

+    view.hook.rerender({ path: '/my-work/notes', key: 'archive' });

+    view.hook.rerender({ path: '/classroom/item/one', key: 'one' });
+    expect(stageRead).not.toHaveBeenCalled(); expect(railRead).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });

+    const view = fixture(); view.reader('one', true, false); view.hook.rerender({ path: '/classroom/item/one', key: 'one' });
+    view.main.dataset.x = '-120'; view.reader('one'); await Promise.resolve();

diff --git a/src/hooks/useNoteReaderTransition.ts b/src/hooks/useNoteReaderTransition.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..31f3330f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/hooks/useNoteReaderTransition.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,193 @@
+import { useLayoutEffect, useRef, type RefObject } from 'react';
+import { useNavigationType } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { gsap } from 'gsap';
+import '@/styles/note-reader-transition.css';
+
+const readerId = (path: string) => {
+  const match = /^\/classroom\/item\/([^/]+)$/.exec(path);
+  if (!match) return null;
+  try { return decodeURIComponent(match[1]); } catch { return null; }
+};
+
+/** One owner for the real secondary rail, stage displacement and note reader. */
+export function useNoteReaderTransition(rootRef: RefObject<HTMLElement>, pathname: string, navigationKey: string) {
+  const navigationType = useNavigationType();
+  const navigateRef = useRef<(path: string, key: string, type: string) => void>();
+  const disposeRef = useRef<() => void>();
+  const ownerRoot = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
+  const currentRoute = useRef({ pathname, navigationKey, navigationType });
+  currentRoute.current = { pathname, navigationKey, navigationType };
+  useLayoutEffect(() => {
+    const root = rootRef.current;
+    if (root === ownerRoot.current) return;
+    disposeRef.current?.();
+    if (!root) return;
+    ownerRoot.current = root;
+    let scope: gsap.Context | undefined;
+    let timeline: gsap.core.Timeline | undefined;
+    let disposed = false;
+    let expectedId: string | null = null;
+    let currentKey = '';
+    let playedKey = '';
+    let shellKey = '';
+    const railSeen = new WeakSet<HTMLElement>();
+    let previousLeft: number | undefined;
+    let previousRail = false;
+    let lastWidth = 0;
+    let viewportWidth = window.innerWidth;
+    let fromLeft: number | undefined;
+    let enteringRail = false;
+    let scrollToReader = false;
+    let animating = false;
+    const media = window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)') ?? { matches: true, addEventListener: undefined, removeEventListener: undefined };
+    const stage = () => root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-note-transition-stage]');
+    const rail = () => root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-note-transition-rail]');
+    const measure = () => {
+      const rect = stage()?.getBoundingClientRect();
+      previousLeft = rect?.left;
+      lastWidth = rect?.width ?? 0;
+      const currentRail = rail();
+      previousRail = !!currentRail && currentRail.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0;
+      viewportWidth = window.innerWidth;
+    };
+    const finish = () => { timeline?.progress(1); };
+    const reset = () => { timeline?.kill(); timeline = undefined; scope?.revert(); scope = undefined; animating = false; root.removeAttribute('data-note-transition-active'); };
+    const play = () => {
+      if (disposed || !expectedId) return;
+      const reader = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-note-reader]')).find(node => node.dataset.noteReader === expectedId);
+      const main = stage();
+      if (!main) return;
+      const confirmed = !!reader || Array.from(rail()?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-note-target-id]') ?? []).some(node => node.dataset.noteTargetId === expectedId);
+      if (!confirmed) {
+        // Item routes also host homework and documents. An unresolved route is
+        // not permission to animate their shell or rewind already painted rail.
+        shellKey = currentKey; enteringRail = false; measure(); fromLeft = previousLeft;
+        return;
+      }
+      const newShell = shellKey !== currentKey;
+      const newReader = !!reader && playedKey !== currentKey;
+      if (!newShell && !newReader) return;
+      if (reader && scrollToReader) { window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'instant' }); scrollToReader = false; }
+      const immediate = media.matches || !!reader?.contains(document.activeElement);
+      if (!immediate) root.setAttribute('data-note-transition-active', '');
+      const paper = rail();
+      const destination = main.getBoundingClientRect();
+      const destinationLeft = destination.left - (scope ? Number(gsap.getProperty(main, 'x')) || 0 : 0);
+      previousLeft = destinationLeft; lastWidth = destination.width;
+      shellKey = currentKey;
+      if (reader) playedKey = currentKey;
+      if (immediate) return;
+      const visibleRail = paper && paper.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0;
+      const railItems = visibleRail ? Array.from(paper.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.classroom-section-heading, .classroom-panel-item')).filter(node => {
+        const rect = node.getBoundingClientRect(), bounds = paper.getBoundingClientRect();
+        return !railSeen.has(node) && rect.height > 0 && rect.bottom > bounds.top && rect.top < Math.min(bounds.bottom, window.innerHeight);
+      }) : [];
+      const blocks = newReader && reader ? Array.from(reader.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-note-reader-block]')).filter(node => node.getBoundingClientRect().height > 0) : [];
+      const animate = () => {
+        const shift = fromLeft !== undefined ? fromLeft - destinationLeft : 0;
+        if (!(newShell && Math.abs(shift) > 1) && !(enteringRail && visibleRail) && !blocks.length) { root.removeAttribute('data-note-transition-active'); return; }
+        animating = true;
+        if (!timeline) timeline = gsap.timeline({ defaults: { ease: 'power3.out' }, onComplete: () => { animating = false; root.removeAttribute('data-note-transition-active'); measure(); } });
+        const at = newShell ? 0 : timeline.time();
+        if (newShell && Math.abs(shift) > 1) timeline.fromTo(main, { x: shift }, { x: 0, duration: 0.52, clearProps: 'transform' }, 0);
+        if (enteringRail && visibleRail) {
+          if (newShell) timeline.fromTo(paper, { x: -24, opacity: 0 }, { x: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 0.5, clearProps: 'transform,opacity' }, 0);
+          if (railItems.length) { railItems.forEach(node => railSeen.add(node)); timeline.fromTo(railItems, { x: -12, opacity: 0 }, { x: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 0.34, stagger: Math.min(0.045, 0.24 / Math.max(1, railItems.length - 1)), clearProps: 'transform,opacity' }, at + 0.09); }
+        }
+        if (blocks.length) timeline.fromTo(blocks, { y: 20, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 0.4, stagger: Math.min(0.06, 0.24 / Math.max(1, blocks.length - 1)), clearProps: 'transform,opacity' }, at + (newShell && enteringRail && visibleRail ? 0.18 : 0));
+        if (!newShell) timeline.play();
+      };
+      if (scope) scope.add(animate); else scope = gsap.context(animate, root);
+    };
+    const synchronizeGeometry = () => {
+      const main = stage();
+      if (!main) return;
+      const currentRail = rail();
+      const hasRail = !!currentRail && currentRail.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0;
+      if (expectedId && !previousRail && hasRail) {
+        // Permissions may mount the secondary rail after the route's first
+        // commit. Invert from the last visual edge before the browser paints.
+        const offset = scope ? Number(gsap.getProperty(main, 'x')) || 0 : 0;
+        fromLeft = previousLeft === undefined ? undefined : previousLeft + offset;
+        finish(); reset();
+        enteringRail = true; previousRail = true; shellKey = '';
+        play();
+        return;
+      }
+      if (!animating) measure();
+    };
+    let observedStage: HTMLElement | null = null;
+    let observedRail: HTMLElement | null = null;
+    const observeGeometry = () => {
+      const main = stage(), paper = rail();
+      if (main === observedStage && paper === observedRail) return;
+      observedStage = main; observedRail = paper;
+      resize?.disconnect(); resize?.observe(root);
+      if (main) resize?.observe(main);
+      if (paper) resize?.observe(paper);
+    };
+    navigateRef.current = (path, key, type) => {
+      if (currentKey === `${key}:${readerId(path) ?? ''}`) return;
+      // Rebase an interrupted transition from its current visual stage position.
+      const currentStage = stage();
+      const offset = currentStage && scope ? Number(gsap.getProperty(currentStage, 'x')) || 0 : 0;
+      fromLeft = previousLeft === undefined ? undefined : previousLeft + offset;
+      reset();
+      expectedId = readerId(path); currentKey = `${key}:${expectedId ?? ''}`;
+      const currentRail = rail();
+      const hasRail = !!currentRail && currentRail.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0;
+      enteringRail = !!expectedId && !previousRail && hasRail;
+      previousRail = hasRail;
+      // Reader PUSH starts at the title. Archive POP/Back retains its own anchor.
+      scrollToReader = !!expectedId && type === 'PUSH';
+      measure();
+      play();
+      observeGeometry();
+    };
+    const mutations = new MutationObserver(() => { synchronizeGeometry(); observeGeometry(); play(); });
+    mutations.observe(root, { subtree: true, childList: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-note-reader', 'data-note-target-id'] });
+    const onResize = () => {
+      const main = stage(), rect = main?.getBoundingClientRect();
+      const width = rect?.width ?? 0;
+      const offset = main && scope ? Number(gsap.getProperty(main, 'x')) || 0 : 0;
+      const layoutLeft = rect ? rect.left - offset : undefined;
+      const currentRail = rail();
+      const hasRail = !!currentRail && currentRail.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0;
+      if (window.innerWidth === viewportWidth) {
+        const sameEdge = previousLeft === undefined || layoutLeft === undefined || Math.abs(layoutLeft - previousLeft) < 1;
+        if (sameEdge && hasRail === previousRail) {
+          // A classic scrollbar can narrow a newly loaded article without
+          // moving its reading edge. Do not truncate that content's reveal.
+          if (Math.abs(width - lastWidth) >= 1) lastWidth = width;
+          if (!animating) measure();
+          observeGeometry(); return;
+        }
+        if (expectedId && !previousRail && hasRail) { synchronizeGeometry(); observeGeometry(); return; }
+      }
+      finish(); reset(); measure();
+      observeGeometry();
+    };
+    const resize = typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined' ? undefined : new ResizeObserver(onResize);
+    observeGeometry(); window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
+    const focus = (event: Event) => {
+      if (event.target instanceof Element && event.target.closest('[data-note-reader], [data-note-transition-rail]')) finish();
+    };
+    const key = (event: KeyboardEvent) => { if (event.key === 'Tab') finish(); };
+    const reduce = () => { if (media.matches) { finish(); reset(); measure(); } };
+    root.addEventListener('focusin', focus); root.addEventListener('keydown', key);
+    media.addEventListener?.('change', reduce);
+    disposeRef.current = () => {
+      disposed = true; navigateRef.current = undefined;
+      ownerRoot.current = null;
+      mutations.disconnect(); resize?.disconnect(); window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
+      root.removeEventListener('focusin', focus); root.removeEventListener('keydown', key);
+      media.removeEventListener?.('change', reduce); reset();
+    };
+    // The root can mount later than the route (profile/auth readiness). Start
+    // that newly mounted owner without making unrelated renders navigation.
+    const route = currentRoute.current;
+    navigateRef.current?.(route.pathname, route.navigationKey, route.navigationType);
+  });
+  useLayoutEffect(() => navigateRef.current?.(pathname, navigationKey, navigationType), [pathname, navigationKey, navigationType]);
+  useLayoutEffect(() => () => disposeRef.current?.(), []);
+}

+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { cleanup, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react';
+const animation = vi.hoisted(() => ({ to: vi.fn(), kill: vi.fn() }));
+vi.mock('gsap', () => ({ gsap: { registerPlugin: vi.fn(), to: animation.to } }));
+vi.mock('gsap/ScrollToPlugin', () => ({ ScrollToPlugin: {} }));
+import { useNotesArchiveScroll } from './useNotesArchiveScroll';
+
+function note(top = 1200) {
+  const target = document.createElement('a');
+  target.scrollIntoView = vi.fn();
+  vi.spyOn(target, 'getBoundingClientRect').mockReturnValue({ top, bottom: top + 100, height: 100 } as DOMRect);
+  return target;
+}
+beforeEach(() => {
+  animation.to.mockReset().mockReturnValue({ kill: animation.kill });
+  animation.kill.mockReset();
+  vi.stubGlobal('matchMedia', vi.fn(() => ({ matches: true })));
+});
+afterEach(() => { cleanup(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); });
+describe('teaching archive return scrolling', () => {
+  it('keeps keyboard and legacy returns immediate', () => {
+    const { result } = renderHook(useNotesArchiveScroll);
+    const target = note();
+    result.current(target, false);
+    expect(target.scrollIntoView).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ block: 'center', behavior: 'instant' });
+    expect(animation.to).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+  it('keeps reduced-motion or coarse-pointer returns immediate', () => {
+    vi.stubGlobal('matchMedia', vi.fn(() => ({ matches: false })));
+    const { result } = renderHook(useNotesArchiveScroll);
+    const target = note();
+    result.current(target, true);
+    expect(target.scrollIntoView).toHaveBeenCalled();
+    expect(animation.to).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+  it('keeps a keyboard-focused return immediate even after a mouse-opened note', () => {
+    const { result } = renderHook(useNotesArchiveScroll);
+    const target = note();
+    vi.spyOn(target, 'matches').mockImplementation((selector) => selector === ':focus-visible');
+    result.current(target, true);
+    expect(target.scrollIntoView).toHaveBeenCalled();
+    expect(animation.to).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+  it('smoothly returns an offscreen mouse-opened note with user interruption enabled', () => {
+    const { result } = renderHook(useNotesArchiveScroll);
+    result.current(note(), true);
+    expect(animation.to).toHaveBeenCalledWith(window, expect.objectContaining({
+      duration: 0.26, scrollTo: expect.objectContaining({ autoKill: true }),
+    }));
+    expect(window.matchMedia).toHaveBeenCalledWith('(pointer: fine) and (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)');
+  });
+  it('does not move a note that is already comfortably visible', () => {
+    const { result } = renderHook(useNotesArchiveScroll);
+    result.current(note(100), true);
+    expect(animation.to).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+  it('cancels its own scroll tween on unmount', () => {
+    const { result, unmount } = renderHook(useNotesArchiveScroll);
+    result.current(note(), true);
+    unmount();
+    expect(animation.kill).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
+  });
+});
diff --git a/src/hooks/useNotesArchiveScroll.ts b/src/hooks/useNotesArchiveScroll.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..c77ebe5e

+++ b/src/hooks/useNotesArchiveScroll.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
+import { gsap } from 'gsap';
+import { ScrollToPlugin } from 'gsap/ScrollToPlugin';
+
+gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollToPlugin);
+
+/** Return to a mouse-opened note without taking over wheel or touch scrolling. */
+export function useNotesArchiveScroll() {
+  const active = useRef<gsap.core.Tween | null>(null);
+  useEffect(() => () => { active.current?.kill(); }, []);
+  return useCallback((target: HTMLElement, pointerReturn: boolean) => {
+    active.current?.kill();
+    active.current = null;
+    const canAnimate = pointerReturn && !target.matches(':focus-visible') && typeof window.matchMedia === 'function'
+      && window.matchMedia('(pointer: fine) and (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)').matches;
+    if (!canAnimate) {
+      target.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'center', behavior: 'instant' });
+      return;
+    }
+    const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
+    if (rect.top >= 24 && rect.bottom <= window.innerHeight - 24) return;
+    const destination = Math.max(0, window.scrollY + rect.top - Math.max(24, (window.innerHeight - rect.height) / 2));
+    active.current = gsap.to(window, {
+      scrollTo: { y: destination, autoKill: true },
+      duration: 0.26,
+      ease: 'power3.out',
+      overwrite: 'auto',
+    });
+  }, []);
+}

+import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
+import { gsap } from 'gsap';
+
+/** The menu alone moves. Keyboard selection and reduced-motion remain immediate. */
+export function useNotesSelectMotion(open: boolean, modality: 'pointer' | 'keyboard') {
+  const media = useRef<ReturnType<typeof gsap.matchMedia> | null>(null);
+  const cleanup = useCallback(() => { media.current?.revert(); media.current = null; }, []);
+  useEffect(() => cleanup, [cleanup]);
+  return useCallback((node: HTMLDivElement | null) => {
+    cleanup();
+    if (!node || !open || modality === 'keyboard') return;
+    const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
+    media.current = mm;
+    mm.add('(prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)', () => {
+      gsap.fromTo(node,
+        { opacity: 0.6, scale: 0.98, y: node.dataset.side === 'top' ? -4 : 4 },
+        { opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0, duration: 0.18, ease: 'power3.out', overwrite: 'auto', clearProps: 'transform,opacity' },
+      );
+    });
+  }, [open, modality, cleanup]);
+}

   switch (item.type) {

+
+export type NotesArchiveOrigin = { id: string; returnTo: string };
+
+/** Carry only an archive's explicit origin through its reader chain. */
+export function notesArchiveOrigin(state: unknown, returnTo: string): NotesArchiveOrigin | null {
+  if (!state || typeof state !== 'object' || Array.isArray(state)) return null;
+  const origin = (state as { notesArchiveOrigin?: unknown }).notesArchiveOrigin;
+  if (!origin || typeof origin !== 'object' || Array.isArray(origin)) return null;
+  const { id, returnTo: path } = origin as Partial<NotesArchiveOrigin>;
+  if (typeof id !== 'string' || !id || id.length > 500 || /[\r\n]/.test(id) || typeof path !== 'string') return null;
+  if (path !== validateNotesReturnTo(path) || path !== validateNotesReturnTo(returnTo)) return null;
+  return { id, returnTo: path };
+}
